```
235: *  爬行者全灭 → 二阶段:瞬移冲刺(3 点环绕) + 幻影(简化:冲刺时半透明)。 */
=== 235 ===
    // 淡入回归（约 30 tick spawnAlpha 归零后回战斗）
    e.ai3++;
    e.ai0 = 0;
    if (e.ai3 >= 30) { e.ai1 = 0; e.ai3 = 0; }
    return;
  }
  if (!player) return;
  e.ai0 += 1;
  if (!e.onGround) {
    e.vy += 0.35; // 重力(原版大跳重力)
    // 空中水平减速朝玩家
    if (e.vx > 0) e.vx -= 0.02; else if (e.vx < 0) e.vx += 0.02;
  } else {
    // 落地:砸压判定 + 30% 出小史莱姆(原版 SpawnNPC(16) 语义)
    if (e.ai0 > 10) {
      const dx = player.cx - e.cx;
      e.vy = -8 - Math.random() * 3; // 大跳(原版 -8~-11)
      e.vx = Math.max(-7, Math.min(7, dx * 0.06));
      if (Math.random() < 0.3) {
        for (let k = 0; k < 2; k++) {
          const s = spawnPart(game, e, 16);
          if (s) { s.x += (Math.random() - 0.5) * 60; s.vy = -4; }
        }
      }
      game.spawnParticles(e.cx, e.y + e.h, '#3080FF', 8, 2.2);
    }
  }
  e.x += e.vx;
  e.y += e.vy;
  // 地形碰撞(实心块)
  collideGround(e, game);
}

/** AI_054 克苏鲁之脑(NPC.cs AI_054 + 267 爬行者环):
 *  一阶段(爬行者存活时无敌:dontTakeDamage)脑体悬空不动,爬行者环绕玩家;
 *  爬行者全灭 → 二阶段:瞬移冲刺(3 点环绕) + 幻影(简化:冲刺时半透明)。 */
export function brainOfCthulhuAI(e: Enemy, game: GameHooks, player: Player | null) {
  // 玩家死/远离（:32557-32566/:32810-32822）：曼哈顿 >6000px 瞬消；
  // 玩家死 → localAI[3] 计数 120，>60 后 vy+=(n-60)*0.25 越坠越快钻地逃离、ai[0]=2、alpha=10
  // （原版还含 !ZoneCrimson 触发——跨群系判定未实现，仅 dead）
  if (player && Math.abs(player.cx - e.cx) + Math.abs(player.cy - e.cy) > 6000) {
    e.dead = true; e.bossFled = true;
    return;
  }
  if (!player) {
    e.encourageDespawn(750); // 原版无显式 EncourageDespawn，靠 CheckActive timeLeft(750) 收尾
    e.ai3++;
    if (e.ai3 > 60) {
      e.vy += (e.ai3 - 60) * 0.25;
      e.ai0 = 2;
      e.spawnAlpha = Math.max(e.spawnAlpha, 245);
    }
    e.x += e.vx; e.y += e.vy;
    return;
  }
  const enemies = game.enemies() as unknown[];
  // 初次:补生成爬行者(原版 371-393 只)——必须先于爬行者计数,
  // 否则首 tick 爬行者为 0 直接落二阶段,永远不生成
  if (!e.bInit) {
    e.bInit = true;
    for (let k = 0; k < 8; k++) {
      const c = spawnPart(game, e, 267);
      if (c) {
        const ang = (k / 8) * Math.PI * 2;
        c.x = player.cx + Math.cos(ang) * 150;
        c.y = player.cy + Math.sin(ang) * 150;
      }
    }
  }
  const creepers = enemies.filter((n) => (n as Enemy).vanillaId === 267 && !(n as Enemy).dead);
  if (creepers.length > 0) {
    // 一阶段:无敌 + 悬浮微移(原版 ai[0]=0 悬空不动;爬行者环攻击)
    e.iframes = 2;
    e.vx *= 0.9; e.vy *= 0.9;
    e.x += e.vx; e.y += e.vy;
    return;
  }
  // 二阶段:瞬移冲刺循环(ai0 计 60t 一冲)
  e.ai0 += 1;
  e.spawnAlpha = 0;
  if (e.ai0 % 60 === 0) {
    // 瞬移到玩家周围随机点,朝玩家冲刺
    const ang = Math.random() * Math.PI * 2;
    e.x = player.cx + Math.cos(ang) * 200 - e.w / 2;
    e.y = player.cy + Math.sin(ang) * 200 - e.h / 2;
    const dx = player.cx - e.cx, dy = player.cy - e.cy;
    const d = Math.hypot(dx, dy) || 1;
    e.vx = (dx / d) * 9; e.vy = (dy / d) * 9;
    game.spawnParticles(e.cx, e.cy, '#A03050', 10, 2);
  }
  e.x += e.vx; e.y += e.vy;
}

/** 267 爬行者(NPC.cs AI_055 简化):环绕主人/玩家漂移 + 接触伤害 */
export function creeperAI(e: Enemy, game: GameHooks, player: Player | null) {
  if (!player) { e.x += e.vx; e.y += e.vy; return; }
  e.ai0 += 0.02;
  // 环绕玩家(半径 100-160 摆动)
  const r = 130 + Math.sin(e.aiT * 0.03) * 30;
  const tx = player.cx - e.w / 2 + Math.cos(e.ai0) * r;
  const ty = player.cy - e.h / 2 + Math.sin(e.ai0) * r;
  e.vx += (tx - e.x) * 0.01;
  e.vy += (ty - e.y) * 0.01;
  e.vx *= 0.92; e.vy *= 0.92;
  e.x += e.vx; e.y += e.vy;
  void game;
}

// ================= 机械三王(NPC.cs AI_030-037,1.4.5.6 行号见各核注释) =================
// 经典模式参数 1:1;专家/大师/FTW 分支仅注释保留。

/** 原版速度逼近(L26539-26670 雏形):朝目标速度单轴步进,符号翻转时双倍步进 */
export function accelTo(v: number, target: number, a: number): number {
  if (v < target) { v += a; if (v < 0 && target > 0) v += a; }
  else if (v > target) { v -= a; if (v > 0 && target < 0) v -= a; }
  return v;
}
/** 朝 (tx,ty) 以速度 spd/加速度 acc 追赶(vanilla numXXX = spd/dist 归一后 accelTo) */
export function seek(e: Enemy, tx: number, ty: number, spd: number, acc: number): void {
  const dx = tx - e.cx, dy = ty - e.cy;
  const d = Math.hypot(dx, dy) || 1;
  e.vx = accelTo(e.vx, (dx / d) * spd, acc);
  e.vy = accelTo(e.vy, (dy / d) * spd, acc);
}
/** 直线视线(Collision.CanHit 1:1,Collision.cs:388-480:逐 tile 扫描,半砖/坡面/
 *  平台/已致动/开门态不阻挡,"两邻格均整实心"=被夹住 → 无视线;8px 采样近似已退役)。
 *  入参为像素点对(w=h=0 → 中心=该点);原版 CanHitLine 场景见 physics/LineOfSight */
export function losClear(game: GameHooks, x0: number, y0: number, x1: number, y1: number): boolean {
  return canHit(game.world.store, x0, y0, 0, 0, x1, y1, 0, 0);
}
/** Boss 弹幕:直飞(grav 0)+ 原版 Projectile_N 贴图。
 *  hostile=true(原版 Projectile.hostile):命中玩家结算——所有走本出口的弹都是
 *  Boss/敌怪发射的敌弹(双子激光/死光/蜂后毒刺/哥布林弹等) */
export function shoot(game: GameHooks, x: number, y: number, vx: number, vy: number, dmg: number, projId: number, life = 300): void {
  (game as unknown as { entities: { add: (e: unknown, b: string) => void } }).entities.add(
    new Arrow(x, y, vx, vy, dmg, 0, projId, null, { grav: 0, life, pierce: 1, hostile: true }), 'projectiles');
}

// Boss AI 1:1 移植(Terarria1456 NPC.cs SetDefaults 指定 aiStyle 的专用核)。
// 由 Enemy.fixedUpdate 的 aiStyle 分发调用;弹幕用 MagicProj/投射桶,部件用 fromVanilla。
// 各核保留原版状态机主链与特征机制(移速/计时/阶段),专家专属分支注释标明。
import type { Enemy } from './Enemy';
import type { GameHooks } from './types';
import type { Player } from './Player';
import { TILE } from '../core/constants';
import { canHit } from '../physics/LineOfSight';
import { MagicProj } from './MagicProj';
import { Arrow } from './Arrow';

const addProj = (game: GameHooks, p: MagicProj) => {
  (game as unknown as { entities: { add: (e: unknown, b: string) => void } }).entities.add(p, 'projectiles');
};
export const addEnemy = (game: GameHooks, e: Enemy) => {
  const em = (game as unknown as { entities?: { nextId?: number; add?: (en: unknown, b: string) => void } }).entities;
  if (em?.nextId !== undefined) e.id = em.nextId++;
  em?.add?.(e, 'enemies');
  return e;
};

/** AI_011 骷髅王战斗状态机(NPC.cs:21904-22288 完整核;守卫分支已在 skeletronHeadAI)。
 *  ai0=初始化(双手 36 生成);ai1: 0=悬浮(800t)→1=旋冲(400t)循环;2=守卫/白天;3=离场。
 *  悬浮: Y 拉向玩家上方 250(0.02/±2),X 朝玩家 0.05/±8;旋冲: rotation+=0.3*dir、
 *  朝玩家 1.5 倍速、伤害 ×1.3、防御 -10(:22026-22179)。 */
export function skeletronBossAI(e: Enemy, game: GameHooks, player: Player | null) {
  const isGuardian = e.vanillaId === 68;
  // 初始化:生成双手 36(:21914-21931)
  if (!e.bInit) {
    e.bInit = true;
    if (!isGuardian) {
      for (const side of [-1, 1]) {
        const h = spawnPart(game, e, 36);
        if (h) { h.ai0 = side; h.ai1 = e.id; h.ai3 = 150; h.bInit = true; }
      }
    }
  }
  // 白天 → ai1=2(守卫/离场语义);玩家死/超 2000px → 3
  if (game.world.clock.isDay && !isGuardian && e.ai1 !== 3) e.ai1 = 2;
  if (!player || Math.abs(e.cx - player.cx) > 2000 || Math.abs(e.cy - player.cy) > 2000) {
    if (!player || Math.abs(e.cx - player.cx) > 2000 || Math.abs(e.cy - player.cy) > 2000) e.ai1 = 3;
  }
  if (e.ai1 === 2) {
    // 白天狂暴（:22247-22261）：damage/defense 均 9999、rotation+=0.3*direction、
    // 8 速恒直追——此前只改了伤害没改防御，且玩家死后 null 解引用
    e.def.damage = 9999;
    e.def.defense = 9999;
    e.spin += (e.facing || 1) * 0.3;
    if (player) {
      const dx = player.cx - e.cx, dy = player.cy - e.cy;
      const d = Math.hypot(dx, dy) || 1;
      e.vx = (dx / d) * 8; e.vy = (dy / d) * 8;
    }
    e.x += e.vx; e.y += e.vy;
    return;
  }
  if (e.ai1 === 3) {
    // 离场（:22262-22271）：vy+=0.1 下坠、vx 衰减、EncourageDespawn(50)——
    // 此前 y+=4 直落且永不 despawn，导致玩家死后 Boss 血条永久残留
    e.vy += 0.1;
    if (e.vy < 0) e.vy *= 0.95;
    e.vx *= 0.95;
    e.x += e.vx; e.y += e.vy;
    e.encourageDespawn(50);
    return;
  }
  if (!player) return;

  e.ai2 += 1;
  if (e.ai1 === 0) {
    // 悬浮段(:22046-22091)
    if (e.ai2 >= 800) { e.ai2 = 0; e.ai1 = 1; }
    // 回悬浮:恢复冲撞前的伤害/防御基值
    const base = baseStats.get(e);
    if (base) { e.def.damage = base.dmg; e.def.defense = base.def; }
    if (e.y > player.y - 250) {
      if (e.vy > 0) e.vy *= 0.98;
      e.vy -= 0.02;
      if (e.vy > 2) e.vy = 2;
    } else {
      if (e.vy < 0) e.vy *= 0.98;
      e.vy += 0.02;
      if (e.vy < -2) e.vy = -2;
    }
    if (e.cx > player.cx) {
      if (e.vx > 0) e.vx *= 0.98;
      e.vx -= 0.05;
      if (e.vx > 8) e.vx = 8;
    } else {
      if (e.vx < 0) e.vx *= 0.98;
      e.vx += 0.05;
      if (e.vx < -8) e.vx = -8;
    }
  } else if (e.ai1 === 1) {
    // 旋冲段(:22115-22179):rot+=0.3*dir,1.5 倍速直冲,伤害 ×1.3,防御 -10。
    // 原版在进入冲撞时一次性应用——每 tick 重乘会把伤害放大 1.3^400,故缓存基值按基值设置
    if (e.ai2 >= 400) { e.ai2 = 0; e.ai1 = 0; }
    let base = baseStats.get(e);
    if (!base) { base = baseOf(e); }
    e.def.defense = Math.max(0, base.def - 10);
    e.def.damage = Math.round(base.dmg * 1.3);
    e.spin += (e.facing || 1) * 0.3;
    const dx = player.cx - e.cx, dy = player.cy - e.cy;
    const d = Math.hypot(dx, dy) || 1;
    const spd = 12; // (1.5x 追击速)
    e.vx += ((dx / d) * spd - e.vx) * 0.1;
    e.vy += ((dy / d) * spd - e.vy) * 0.1;
  }
  e.facing = e.vx > 0 ? 1 : -1;
  e.x += e.vx; e.y += e.vy;
}

/** Boss 伤害/防御基值缓存(状态机每 tick 从基值派生临时修正,防重复乘膨胀) */
const baseStats = new WeakMap<Enemy, { dmg: number; def: number }>();
const baseOf = (e: Enemy) => {
  let b = baseStats.get(e);
  if (!b) { b = { dmg: e.def.damage, def: e.def.defense ?? 0 }; baseStats.set(e, b); }
  return b;
};

let enemyCtor: (typeof import('./Enemy'))['Enemy'] | null = null;
/** Enemy 构造注入(Enemy.ts import 时回填,避免循环依赖) */
export function bindEnemyCtor(c: (typeof import('./Enemy'))['Enemy']) { enemyCtor = c; }

export function spawnPart(game: GameHooks, src: Enemy, id: number): Enemy | null {
  if (!enemyCtor) return null;
  const p = enemyCtor.fromVanilla(id, src.cx, src.cy);
  if (!p) return null;
  return addEnemy(game, p);
}

/** AI_012 骷髅王手(NPC.cs:22289-22400):锚定头(ai1=头 id),头离场 → 自毁;
 *  头非悬浮态 → 漂在头两侧(±120×ai0, 头上方 100);悬浮态 → 缓慢环绕(±200, +230)。
 *  每 300t(ai3 计)朝玩家挥击冲刺。 */
export function skeletronHandAI(e: Enemy, game: GameHooks, player: Player | null) {
  const headId = e.ai1;
  const enemies = game.enemies() as unknown[];
  const head = enemies.find((n) => (n as Enemy).id === headId) as Enemy | undefined;
  if (!head || head.vanilla?.aiStyle !== 11 || head.dead) { e.dead = true; return; }
  e.master = head; // 臂骨 IK 渲染用（Renderer.drawSkeletronArm）

```

      // :26264-26296 眼激光 83:速 9 / 伤 11 起,低血加档(:26268-26276)
      let num410 = 9;

      if (head.hp < head.maxHp * 0.1) { num411 += 2; num410 += 2; }
      const dx = player.cx - e.cx, dy = player.cy - e.cy;
      const d = Math.hypot(dx, dy) || 1;
      const vx = (dx / d) * num410, vy = (dy / d) * num410;
      // 出膛点 = 眼中心 + 单位速度(:26278-26286);83 原版 timeLeft 600/extraUpdates 2
      // (Projectile.cs:1360-1370),本仓 Arrow 未建模 extraUpdates,按原速直飞
      shoot(game, e.cx + vx, e.cy + vy, vx, vy, num411, 83, 600);

  // 移动自行积分:X 已钉墙,仅 Y 步进

// ================= aiStyle 29 饥饿者 115(NPC.cs:26299-26496) =================

/** ai0 = 沿空腔竖向的挂点比例(墙生成时 -0.05..0.95 均布),ai1 = 脱落倒计时(0=挂墙),

 *  挂墙(ai1==0):绕锚点(墙中心 X, 空腔顶 + 跨度×ai0)以 ±0.1 步进追"锚点 + 朝玩家

export function hungryAI(e: Enemy, game: GameHooks, player: Player | null) {
  // :26299-26301 justHit → ai[1]=10(Enemy 无 justHit 字段,以受击无敌帧近似:hurt 置

  // :26308-26335 挂墙半径/伤害/防御随墙血量变化(经典档;expert 的 defense 恢复与
  //        槽位倍率 :26337-26358 不实现)
  let num414 = 0.1;   // 步进
  let num415 = 300;   // 挂墙半径
  const b = baseOf(e);
  e.def.damage = b.dmg; // :26311 damage = defDamage(每 tick 复位)
  let num416 = 0;
  if (head.hp < head.maxHp * 0.5) {
    num416 = 60;
    e.def.defense = 30;
    num415 = 700;
  } else if (head.hp < head.maxHp * 0.75) {
    num416 = 45;
    e.def.defense = 20;
    num415 = 500;
  } else {
    e.def.defense = b.def;
  }
  if (num416 > 0) e.def.damage = num416; // :26333-26335 GetAttackDamage_ScaledByDifficulty 经典=原值

export function enemySpawnChain(head: Enemy, count: number): Enemy[] {

/** 头驱动段链收缩跟随(原版 :50374-50414,linkDist 可配) */
export function driveChain(head: Enemy, linkDist: number): void {
  for (let s = head.wormNext; s; s = s.wormNext) {
    const fx = s.wormFollow!;
    const dxC = fx.cx - s.cx;
    const dyC = fx.cy - s.cy;
    const dist = Math.hypot(dxC, dyC);
    if (dist > 0.01) {
      const shrink = (dist - linkDist) / dist;
      s.x += dxC * shrink;
      s.y += dyC * shrink;
      s.spin = Math.atan2(dyC, dxC) + Math.PI / 2;
      s.facing = dxC < 0 ? 1 : -1;
    }
  }
}

/** 朝玩家发射一发弹幕的通用出口(伤害/弹 id 全部来自原版 NewProjectile 调用点) */
function mlShoot(e: Enemy, game: GameHooks, player: Player, ox: number, oy: number,
  spd: number, dmg: number, projId: number) {
  const dx = player.cx - (e.cx + ox), dy = player.cy - (e.cy + oy);
  const d = Math.hypot(dx, dy) || 1;
  shoot(game, e.cx + ox, e.cy + oy, (dx / d) * spd, (dy / d) * spd, dmg, projId, 600);
}

/** checkDead(:78845-78857):手 397 / 头 396 血尽 → ai0=-2 闭眼残肢的同时,于部件中心
 *  召 NPC 400 真眼,ai[3] = 部件的 ai[3](核心 id,游离眼 AI 据此锚定核心) */
function spawnTrueEye(game: GameHooks, part: Enemy): void {
  const eye = spawnPart(game, part, 400);
  if (!eye) return;
  eye.x = part.cx - eye.w / 2;
  eye.y = part.cy - eye.h / 2;
  eye.ai0 = 0; eye.ai1 = 0; eye.ai2 = 0; eye.ai3 = part.ai3;
}

/** 月噬弹 456（Projectile.cs:5008 SetDefaults + aiStyle 85 :30678-30750）：
 *  16×16 / hostile / 0 伤 / noTileCollide / timeLeft 600。ai[0]>0 出击段：每 tick 朝
 *  玩家 min(16, 距离) 直飞，贴身 <20px 时 AddBuff(145 MoonLeech, 840t/专家 960t)
 *  （:30722-30732，本仓经典模式恒 840t = 14s）；age≥330 或玩家失联 → ai[0] 翻负
 *  回程段：朝 额前(0,+216) 直飞，<20px Kill（吸血视觉闭环）。
 *  弹体自旋角 = (源心 - 玩家心 + (0,216)).ToRotation() + π/2（:30717）。 */
export class MoonLeechProj extends Entity {
  w = 16; h = 16;
  vx = 0; vy = 0;
  private srcId: number;
  private out = true;      // ai[0] 符号：true = 出击 / false = 回程

```

  /** 商店上架门（Chest.SetupShop if 门 → 本作状态映射；未实现事件门不上架） */
  private shopCondOk(cond: string | null): boolean {
    if (!cond) return true;
    // 复合门（extract-shops.mjs parseGate：嵌套 if 的全部开门按 '&&' 连接，
    // 如 `hardMode && ZoneGraveyard`——外层与内层同时成立才上架）
    if (cond.includes('&&')) return cond.split('&&').every((c) => this.shopCondOk(c));
    const f = this.world.flags;
    switch (cond) {
      case 'hardMode': return !!f.hardMode;
      case 'downedBoss1': return !!(f['downed_4'] || f.downedEyeOfCthulhu);
      case 'downedBoss2': return !!(f['downed_13'] || f['downed_266']);
      case 'downedBoss3': return !!(f['downed_35'] || f.downedSkeletron);
      case 'downedMechBossAny':
        return !!(f['downed_125'] || f['downed_126'] || f['downed_127'] || f['downed_134']);
      case 'moonPhase':
        // 月相门：extract-shops.mjs 未捕获 case 相位值——恒 false，分档上架由
        // shopStockFor 的相位实表负责（改 true 会全相位重复上架）
        return false;
      case 'night': return !this.world.clock.isDay;
      case 'day': return this.world.clock.isDay;
      case 'zoneSnow': return !!this.scene?.zoneSnow;
      case 'zoneJungle': return !!this.scene?.zoneJungle;
      case 'zoneGraveyard': return !!this.scene?.zoneGraveyard;
      case 'zoneMeteor': return !!this.scene?.zoneMeteor;
      case 'zoneUnderworld': return !!this.scene?.zoneUnderworldHeight;
      case 'crimson': return this.world.crimson;
      case 'bloodMoon': return this.world.clock.bloodMoon;
      case 'xMas': return this.world.clock.xMas;
      case 'eclipse': return !!this.world.clock.eclipse; // 日食（电子人 774 等）
      case 'party': return partyIsUp(this.partyState);   // 派对进行中（派对商品族）
      case 'lanternNight': return LanternNight.lanternsUp; // 灯笼夜（派对女孩 4702 放飞灯笼）
      default: return false;
    }
  }

  // ---- LanternNight 上下文（LanternNight.cs 静态字段读取点 → 本仓世界态映射）----

  /** LanternCtx：LanternsCanStart/Persist 的五门 + BossIsActive（:59-79） */
  private lanternCtx() {
    const w = this.world;
    return {
      bloodMoon: !!w.clock.bloodMoon,
      pumpkinMoon: w.moonEvent.kind === MOON_KIND_PUMPKIN,
      snowMoon: w.moonEvent.kind === MOON_KIND_FROST,
      invasionType: w.invasionType,
      moonLordCountdown: w.lunarEvent.moonLordCountdown,
      // WorldGen.spawnMeteor——本仓陨石坠落系统未移植恒 false（登记：spawnMeteor 位）
      spawnMeteor: false,
      downedMoonlord: !!w.flags['downed_398'],
      // BossIsActive（:68-79）：boss 或 13-15（世吞体节/克脑爬行者）——本仓 Boss 单槽
      // + 入场怪近似为「Boss 槽在场或世吞体节/爬行者在场」
      bossActive: () => {
        if (this.boss && !this.boss.dead) return true;
        return this.entities.enemies.some((n) => {
          const en = n as Enemy;
          return !en.dead && en.vanillaId != null && en.vanillaId >= 13 && en.vanillaId <= 15;
        });
      },
    };
  }

  /** 灯笼夜概率/状态入口（供调试与对话/天气消费方读） */
  get lanternsUp(): boolean { return LanternNight.lanternsUp; }

  private openNpcShop() {
    const npc = this.dialogNpc;
    if (!npc) return;
    const entries = this.shopStockFor(npc).map(([vid, price]) => ({
      key: VANILLA_ITEM_KEY_BY_ID[vid] ?? '',
      vanillaId: vid,
      name: Lang.itemName(vid) ?? VANILLA_ITEM_KEY_BY_ID[vid] ?? String(vid),
      price,
    }));
    this.cb.onNpcShop?.(Lang.inter(28), entries, this.coinsOwned());
  }

  reforgeCost(item: { id: number; stack: number }): number {

    return this.player.equipStats.discount ? Math.max(1, Math.floor(cost * 0.8)) : cost;
  }

    if (vid < 0) return false;
    return rollablePrefixes(vid, !!def.accessory) !== null;
  }

```
// 播种扫描器(Terrarria1456 Terraria.Graphics.Light/TileLightScanner.cs 一比一主干)。
// GetTileMask(:68-87):遮挡分类;GetTileLight(:89-108):逐格 max-raise 播种
// (天空 ApplySurfaceLight:3152 → 地狱 ApplyHellLight:3261 → 墙 :167 → tile :344 → 液体 :110)。
// 洞穴(worldSurface..UnderworldLayer)零环境光。
import { TILE_DEFS } from '../data/tiles';
import { lightIsOn } from '../world/wiring/devices';
import { TileStore } from '../world/TileStore';
import { FastRandom } from './FastRandom';
import { CRACKED_SHEETS, FLICKER_SHEETS, MUSHROOM_GLOW_SHEETS, TORCH_COLORS, WALL_EMISSIVE, WALL_LIGHT } from './lightTables';
import { FlickerClock, skySeed } from './SkyColor';
import { LightMap, Mask } from './LightMap';

/** 扫描器依赖的最小世界结构(引擎注入;World 满足该接口) */
export interface ScannerWorld {
  store: TileStore;
  groundLevel: number;  // 原版 worldSurface
  lavaLine: number;     // 原版 UnderworldLayer
  clock: { timeOfDay: number; dayCount: number; eclipse?: boolean };
}

/** 动态点光(原版 Lighting.AddLight;0-1 RGB) */
export interface PerFrameLight { x: number; y: number; r: number; g: number; b: number }

/**
 * 动态发光特例（原版 ApplyTileLight 帧条件/动态色 case 1:1）：
 * - 126 迪斯科球：frameX<36 → Disco RGB（TileLightScanner.cs:3057）
 * - 429 导线灯泡：frameX/18 位段 → 各线色 +0.5、致动器 +0.2R/G（cs:881-905）
 * - 209 传送门炮：frameX 234/252（紫）306/324（橙）×0.65（cs:432，PortalHelper 色近似）
 * - 84 开花药草：style2 死亡草随闪烁暗红 / style5 火焰草橙 / style6 颤骨草淡蓝（cs:3009）
 */
export function specialTileLight(
  sheet: number, frameX: number, frameY: number, flicker: FlickerClock,
): [number, number, number] | null {
  switch (sheet) {
    case 42: {
      // 灯笼(TileLightScanner 主 switch case 42):frameX!=0(灭档 18)不发光;
      // 光色按样式行 frameY/36 —— def.light 静态单色由 lantern def 移除,全走此表
      if (frameX !== 0) return null;
      const LANTERN_LIGHT: Record<number, [number, number, number]> = {
        0: [0.7, 0.65, 0.55], 1: [0.9, 0.75, 0.6], 2: [0.8, 0.6, 0.6],
        3: [0.65, 0.5, 0.2], 4: [0.5, 0.7, 0.4], 5: [0.9, 0.4, 0.2],
        6: [0.7, 0.75, 0.3], 7: [0.8, 0.8, 0.8], 8: [0.75, 0.6, 0.3],
        9: [0.9, 0.2, 0.4], 11: [0.85, 0.6, 1], 14: [1, 0.95, 0.65],
      };
      // 样式 7/9 原版叠加 demonTorch 调制(Main.demonTorch 全局比值)——
      // 恶魔火把计数系统未实装,取中值 0.5 近似
      return LANTERN_LIGHT[Math.floor(frameY / 36)] ?? [0.7, 0.65, 0.55];
    }
    case 126:
      if (frameX < 36) {
        const [r, g, b] = flicker.discoColor();
        return [r, g, b];
      }
      return null;
    case 429: {
      const n = Math.floor(frameX / 18);
      let r = 0, g = 0, b = 0;
      if (n % 2 >= 1) r += 0.5;
      if (n % 4 >= 2) g += 0.5;
      if (n % 8 >= 4) b += 0.5;
      if (n % 16 >= 8) { r += 0.2; g += 0.2; }
      return [r, g, b];
    }
    case 209:
      if (frameX === 234 || frameX === 252) return [0.42, 0.2, 0.65];   // 传送门 1 紫 ×0.65
      if (frameX === 306 || frameX === 324) return [0.65, 0.26, 0];     // 传送门 2 橙 ×0.65
      return null;
    case 84: {
      const style = Math.floor(frameX / 18);
      if (style === 2) {
        const n = Math.max(0, Math.min(1, (270 - flicker.mouseTextColor) / 400));
        return [n * 1.4, n * 1.2, n / 2];
      }
      if (style === 5) return [0.9, 0.72, 0.18];
      if (style === 6) return [0, 0.08 * 0.8, 0.08];
      return null;
    }
    default:
      return null;
  }
}

export class TileLightScanner {
  private blocksLight: Uint8Array;
  private random = new FastRandom(0x9e3779b9);
  /** 每格局部随机(WithModifier(x,y);蜡烛闪烁) */
  private localRandom(x: number, y: number): FastRandom {
    return this.random.withModifier((y * 2654435761 + x) | 0);
  }

```

And num3:
```ts
// 目标相对本体底边的高差换算格数（cs:48292-48303）：zero = 目标中心 − 本体底边，
// 目标在上(zero.Y<0)时 num3 = (int)zero.Y / -16；朝向与目标方位不一致则归 0

```
Careful with `(int)zero.Y / -16`: C# `(int)zero.Y` truncates toward zero, then integer division by -16 truncates toward zero. zero.Y < 0 → (int)zero.Y negative; / -16 → positive. E.g. zero.Y = -100 → 100/16 = 6.25 → 6. So num3 = Math.trunc(zy) / -16 with integer division → `Math.trunc(Math.trunc(zy) / -16)`. I'll compute `const iy = Math.trunc(zy); num3 = Math.trunc(iy / -16);`.

Then probe depth num8 = 8 + num3, and near threshold `i < num7 + 5 + num3`.

And follow branch gate: `num2 < 400 && Collision.CanHit(...)` — currently pD < 400 without CanHit. Vanilla includes CanHit(position,w,h, player.position,w,h). We have canHit imported in Enemy? Enemy.ts uses `this.canSeePlayer(st, player)` in places. For balloons (noTileCollide? windy balloon flies, no tile collide), CanHit matters. Let me use `this.canSeePlayer` — check its signature.

Then the follow Y uses `base.Center.Y + height/4` vs player `position.Y + height/4`: our current `this.cy + this.h/4 > player!.cy` — player.cy is center, vanilla uses player.position.Y + height/4 (i.e. center - height/4). Should fix to `player.cy - player.h/4`. Hmm, that's another drift but within the same block.     // (expert 1/100, normal 1/200) → dart at player 8 speed, ai[0]=1
    if ((expert && rand(100)==0) || rand(200)==0) {
        TargetClosest; velocity = dir*8; ai[0]=1
    }
    return;
}
// ai[0]==1 (attacking):
if (expertMode) { homing at player: vel = (vel*99 + dir*9)/100 (classic-else branch: vel*99/100 + dir*9/100) }
// check distance to boss
dx = boss.cx - cx ...
if (d > 700) ai[0] = 0;
else {
    if (!justHit) return;
    if (knockBackResist == 0) { ai[1]++; if (ai[1] > 5) ai[0] = 0; }
    else ai[0] = 0;
}
```

Note: in classic mode ai[0]==1: velocity stays whatever it was (dart straight) — no homing. And if distance ≤700: if !justHit → return (keep flying straight); on justHit: if knockBackResist==0 (immune to knockback), count hits and after >5 (6 hits) return to orbit; else return to orbit immediately.

}

193:  playFiles(files: string[], vol = 1, x?: number, y?: number): boolean {
  playFiles(files: string[], vol = 1, x?: number, y?: number): boolean {
    if (!files.length) return false;
    return this.playWavFile(files[Math.floor(Math.random() * files.length)], vol, x, y);
  }

  /** 按文件名直播（缓冲未就位时触发后台加载，本播返回 false 走合成兜底） */
  playWavFile(file: string, vol = 1, x?: number, y?: number): boolean {
    const ctx = this.ensure();
    if (!ctx) return false;
    const v = this.atten(vol, x, y);
    if (v <= 0) return false;
    vol = v;
    const buf = this.ensureBuffer(file);
    if (!buf) return false;
    const src = ctx.createBufferSource();
    src.buffer = buf;
    src.playbackRate.value = 0.92 + Math.random() * 0.16;
    const g = ctx.createGain();
    g.gain.value = Math.min(1, vol * this.master);
    src.connect(g).connect(ctx.destination);
    src.start();

```

294	export function creeperAI(e: Enemy, game: GameHooks, player: Player | null) {

296	  e.ai0 += 0.02;
297	  // 环绕玩家(半径 100-160 摆动)

300	  const ty = player.cy - e.h / 2 + Math.sin(e.ai0) * r;
301	  e.vx += (tx - e.x) * 0.01;
302	  e.vy += (ty - e.y) * 0.01;

e.vy *= 0.9;\n    e.x += e.vx; e.y += e.vy;\n    return;\n  }\n  // 二阶段:瞬移冲刺循环(ai0 计 60t 一冲)\n  e.ai0 += 1;\n  e.spawnAlpha = 0;\n  if (e.ai0 % 60 === 0) {\n    // 瞬移到玩家周围随机点,朝玩家冲刺\n    const ang = Math.random() * Math.PI * 2;\n    e.x = player.cx + Math.cos(ang) * 200 - e.w / 2;\n    e.y = player.cy + Math.sin(ang) * 200 - e.h / 2;\n    const dx = player.cx - e.cx, dy = player.cy - e.cy;\n    const d = Math.hypot(dx, dy) || 1;\n    e.vx = (dx / d) * 9; e.vy = (dy / d) * 9;\n    game.spawnParticles(e.cx, e.cy, '#A03050', 10, 2);\n  }\n  e.x += e.vx; e.y += e.vy;\n}\n\n/** 267 爬行者(NPC.cs AI_055 简化):环绕主人/玩家漂移 + 接触伤害 */\nexport function creeperAI(e: Enemy, game: GameHooks, player: Player | null) {\n  if (!player) { e.x += e.vx; e.y += e.vy; return; }\n  e.ai0 += 0.02;\n  // 环绕玩家(半径 100-160 摆动)\n  const r = 130 + Math.sin(e.aiT * 0.03) * 30;\n  const tx = player.cx - e.w / 2 + Math.cos(e.ai0) * r;\n  const ty = player.cy - e.h / 2 + Math.sin(e.ai0) * r;\n  e.vx += (tx - e.x) * 0.01;\n  e.vy += (ty - e.y) * 0.01;\n  e.vx *= 0.92; e.vy *= 0.92;\n  e.x += e.vx; e.y += e.vy;\n  void game;\n}",
 "new_string": "/** AI_054 克苏鲁之脑 266（NPC.cs:32537-32827 全核 1:1）：\n *  SetDefaults :12370-12386 dontTakeDamage=true（fromVanilla 已置位）→ 一阶段不可击；\n *  爬行者清零 → ai[0]=-1 进二阶段（入场演出一次性 + 解除无敌）。\n *  一阶段 ai[0]：0 缓追玩家（1 速 :32703-32723）+ 瞬移计时 localAI[1] 达\n *  120+Next(300) 选点(:32744-32785，12..40 格偏移、目标速度向投影 +16×speed、\n *  101 次后无条件 / 非实心且(>75 次或 CanHit) 才收)→ 1 渐隐(+5/t 到 255)→\n *  落点瞬移(:32788-32799)→ 2 渐显(-5/t 到 0)→ 回 0。\n *  二阶段 ai[0]<0：-1 追踪 8 速 ((v*50+dir)/51 :32591-32600)，计时 localAI[1]\n *  达 60+Next(120)（justHit 时 -Next(5) :32605-32609）→ -2（v×0.9、ai[3]+=25/t\n *  到 255 后瞬移 :32660-32681）→ -3（ai[3]-=25/t 回 -1 :32682-32699），alpha 跟 ai[3]。\n *  注：原版**无幻影分身机制**——所谓\"幻影\"即这套 alpha 渐隐/渐显瞬移循环\n *  （旧\"冲刺时半透明\"近似已退役）。\n *  距离消散 :32555-32567（曼哈顿 >6000px）；玩家死/离猩红 :32810-32826\n *  （localAI[3] 钳 120，>60 后 vy+=(n-60)*0.25、ai[0]=2、alpha=10；\n *  ZoneCrimson 未建模仅 dead 触发——登记）。 */\nexport function brainOfCthulhuAI(e: Enemy, game: GameHooks, player: Player | null) {\n  const st = game.world.store;\n  // :32555-32567 TargetClosest 后曼哈顿 >6000 → active=false（记逃走）\n  if (player && Math.abs(player.cx - e.cx) + Math.abs(player.cy - e.cy) > 6000) {\n    e.dead = true; e.bossFled = true;\n    return;\n  }\n  // 初次生成爬行者（:32540-32553）：GetBrainOfCthuluCreepersCount(:51330)=20\n  // （getGoodWorld 40 不建模）；出点 = 本体中心 ±(width/height) 随机，\n  // 初速 = Next(-30,31)*0.1。爬行者锚定本体（原版 crimsonBoss 静态位 → master 引用）\n  if (!e.bInit) {\n    e.bInit = true;\n    for (let k = 0; k < 20; k++) {\n      const c = spawnPart(game, e, 267);\n      if (!c) continue;\n      c.master = e;\n      c.x = e.cx + (Math.floor(Math.random() * (2 * e.w)) - e.w) - c.w / 2;\n      c.y = e.cy + (Math.floor(Math.random() * (2 * e.h)) - e.h) - c.h / 2;\n      c.vx = (Math.floor(Math.random() * 61) - 30) * 0.1;\n      c.vy = (Math.floor(Math.random() * 61) - 30) * 0.1;\n    }\n  }\n  // :32810-32826 玩家死（ZoneCrimson 未建模）→ 下坠逃离：计数钳 120，>60 越坠越快\n  const playerGone = !player || player.dead;\n  if (playerGone) {\n    if (e.lai3 < 120) e.lai3 += 1;\n    if (e.lai3 > 60) e.vy += (e.lai3 - 60) * 0.25;\n    e.ai0 = 2;\n    e.spawnAlpha = 10;\n  } else if (e.lai3 > 0) {\n    e.lai3 -= 1;\n  }\n  // 一阶段爬行者计数（:32726-32742）\n  const creepers = (game.enemies() as Enemy[]).filter((n) => !n.dead && n.vanillaId === 267);\n\n  if (e.ai0 < 0) {\n    // ---- 二阶段（:32569-32699）----\n    // :32575-32588 入场演出一次性（localAI[2] 门）：SoundID 3（Zombie_1）+\n    //   血块 gore 392-395（gore 未接 → 粒子近似，登记）+ 尘 5 号 ×20 + SoundID 15\n    //   （ZombieMoan wav 未提取 → 略，登记）\n    if (e.lai2 === 0) {\n      e.lai2 = 1;\n      game.playSfxFiles(['Zombie_1'], 1, e.cx, e.cy);\n      game.spawnParticles(e.cx, e.cy, '#A03050', 20, 2);\n      game.playSfxFiles(['Zombie_2'], 0.8, e.cx, e.cy);\n    }\n    // :32589 爬行者全灭后解除一阶段无敌（SetDefaults :12372 置位）\n    e.dontTakeDamage = false;\n    // :32590-32600 追踪：8 速逼近（(v*50+dir)/51）\n    if (player) {\n      const dx = player.cx - e.cx, dy = player.cy - e.cy;\n      const d = Math.hypot(dx, dy) || 1;\n      e.vx = (e.vx * 50 + (dx / d) * 8) / 51;\n      e.vy = (e.vy * 50 + (dy / d) * 8) / 51;\n    }\n    if (e.ai0 === -1) {\n      // :32603-32657 瞬移计时：localAI[1]+1，justHit 抵扣 Next(5)；\n      //   达 60+Next(120)（单人无 netMode 附加段 :32611-32614）→ 选点 → ai[0]=-2\n      e.lai1 += 1;\n      if (e.justHit) e.lai1 -= Math.floor(Math.random() * 5);\n      const num859 = 60 + Math.floor(Math.random() * 120);\n      if (e.lai1 >= num859 && player) {\n        e.lai1 = 0;\n        const spot = pickTeleportSpot(st, e, player, 10, 12, false);\n        e.ai3 = 0;\n        e.ai0 = -2;\n        e.ai1 = spot.x; e.ai2 = spot.y;\n      }\n    } else if (e.ai0 === -2) {\n      // :32660-32681 渐隐：v×0.9，ai[3]+=25/t（单人档），到 255 → 落点瞬移\n      e.vx *= 0.9; e.vy *= 0.9;\n      e.ai3 += 25;\n      if (e.ai3 >= 255) {\n        e.ai3 = 255;\n        e.x = e.ai1 * TILE - e.w / 2;\n        e.y = e.ai2 * TILE - e.h / 2;\n        game.playSfxFiles(['Item_8'], 1, e.cx, e.cy); // SoundID.Item8\n        e.ai0 = -3;\n      }\n      e.spawnAlpha = e.ai3;\n    } else if (e.ai0 === -3) {\n      // :32682-32699 渐显：ai[3]-=25/t，≤0 回 -1\n      e.ai3 -= 25;\n      if (e.ai3 <= 0) {\n        e.ai3 = 0;\n        e.ai0 = -1;\n      }\n      e.spawnAlpha = e.ai3;\n    }\n  } else {\n    // ---- 一阶段（:32701-32808）----\n    // :32703-32723 缓追玩家：1 速（getGoodWorld ×3 不建模），<1px 直达\n    if (player) {\n      let dx = player.cx - e.cx, dy = player.cy - e.cy;\n      const num870 = 1;\n      const d = Math.hypot(dx, dy);\n      if (d < num870) {\n        e.vx = dx; e.vy = dy;\n      } else {\n        e.vx = (dx / d) * num870;\n        e.vy = (dy / d) * num870;\n      }\n    }\n    if (e.ai0 === 0) {\n      // :32726-32742 爬行者清零 → 二阶段\n      if (creepers.length === 0) {\n        e.ai0 = -1;\n        e.lai1 = 0;\n        e.spawnAlpha = 0;\n      }\n      // :32743-32785 瞬移计时：120+Next(300) → 选点（12..40 格）→ ai[0]=1\n      if (player) {\n        e.lai1 += 1;\n        if (e.lai1 >= 120 + Math.floor(Math.random() * 300)) {\n          e.lai1 = 0;\n          const spot = pickTeleportSpot(st, e, player, 12, 40, true);\n          e.ai0 = 1;\n          e.ai1 = spot.x; e.ai2 = spot.y;\n        }\n      }\n    } else if (e.ai0 === 1) {\n      // :32788-32799 渐隐：+5/t 到 255 → 落点瞬移 → ai[0]=2\n      e.spawnAlpha = Math.min(255, e.spawnAlpha + 5);\n      if (e.spawnAlpha >= 255) {\n        game.playSfxFiles(['Item_8'], 1, e.cx, e.cy); // SoundID.Item8\n        e.x = e.ai1 * TILE - e.w / 2;\n        e.y = e.ai2 * TILE - e.h / 2;\n        e.ai0 = 2;\n      }\n    } else if (e.ai0 === 2) {\n      // :32800-32808 渐显：-5/t 到 0 → ai[0]=0\n      e.spawnAlpha = Math.max(0, e.spawnAlpha - 5);\n      if (e.spawnAlpha <= 0) e.ai0 = 0;\n    }\n  }\n  e.x += e.vx;\n  e.y += e.vy;\n}\n\n/** 克脑瞬移选点（一阶段 :32748-32784 / 二阶段 :32620-32656 同构，参数不同）：\n *  以玩家所在格为原点，min..max 格随机偏移（各 50% 取负）；偏移向量与玩家速度\n *  同向（点积>0）时沿自身方向外推 16×speed px；一阶段收点条件 =\n *  101 次后无条件 或（非实心 且（76 次后 或 对玩家 CanHit）），二阶段 =\n *  101 次后无条件 或 非实心。返回 tile 坐标。 */\nfunction pickTeleportSpot(\n  st: GameHooks['world']['store'], _e: Enemy, p: Player,\n  min: number, max: number, losGate: boolean,\n): { x: number; y: number } {\n  const pxSpeed = Math.hypot(p.vx, p.vy);\n  let tries = 0;\n  let tx = Math.floor(p.cx / TILE), ty = Math.floor(p.cy / TILE);\n  for (;;) {\n    tries++;\n    let a = min + Math.floor(Math.random() * (max - min + 1));\n    let b = min + Math.floor(Math.random() * (max - min + 1));\n    if (Math.floor(Math.random() * 2) === 0) a *= -1;\n    if (Math.floor(Math.random() * 2) === 0) b *= -1;\n    let vx = a * TILE, vy = b * TILE;\n    // 玩家速度与偏移同向 → 外推（SafeNormalize 后 ×16×speed）\n    if (p.vx * vx + p.vy * vy > 0) {\n      const vl = Math.hypot(vx, vy) || 1;\n      vx += (vx / vl) * 16 * pxSpeed;\n      vy += (vy / vl) * 16 * pxSpeed;\n    }\n    tx = Math.floor(p.cx / TILE) + Math.trunc(vx / TILE);\n    ty = Math.floor(p.cy / TILE) + Math.trunc(vy / TILE);\n    if (st.inBounds(tx, ty)) {\n      const solid = st.isSolid(tx, ty); // WorldGen.SolidTile\n      const canSee = losGate && canHit(st, tx * TILE, ty * TILE, 1, 1, p.x, p.y, p.w, p.h);\n      if (tries > 100 || (!solid && (!losGate || tries > 75 || canSee))) break;\n    } else if (tries > 100) break;\n  }\n  return { x: tx, y: ty };\n}\n\n/** 267 爬行者（NPC.cs:32828-32914 全核 1:1，SetDefaults :12388-12400）：\n *  ai[0]=0 环绕态：距主体 >90px 时 8 速逼近（(v*15+dir)/16）后 return；\n *  ≤90px 且 |v|<8 时两轴 ×1.05；掷 1/200（专家 1/100）→ 8 速直冲玩家、ai[0]=1。\n *  ai[0]=1 扑咬态：经典档无追踪（专家 (v*99+dir*9)/100 :32872-32886）；\n *  距主体 >700px 回环绕；否则 !justHit 即 return（保持直飞），\n *  justHit 时按 knockBackResist 分流（=0 → 计数 ai[1] 超 5 回环绕，\n *  否则立即回环绕——267 原值 0.8 → 一击即回）。主体不在 → 消散（:32830-32835）。 */\nexport function creeperAI(e: Enemy, game: GameHooks, _player: Player | null) {\n  const boss = e.master;\n  // :32830-32835 crimsonBoss<0（主体不在）→ active=false\n  if (!boss || boss.dead || boss.vanillaId !== 266) { e.dead = true; return; }\n  const player = _player && !_player.dead ? _player : null;\n  if (e.ai0 === 0) {\n    // :32836-32856 环绕态：ai[1]=0；距主体 >90px 逼近\n    e.ai1 = 0;\n    const dx = boss.cx - e.cx, dy = boss.cy - e.cy;\n    const d = Math.hypot(dx, dy);\n    if (d > 90) {\n      e.vx = (e.vx * 15 + (dx / d) * 8) / 16;\n      e.vy = (e.vy * 15 + (dy / d) * 8) / 16;\n      e.x += e.vx; e.y += e.vy;\n      return;\n    }\n    if (Math.abs(e.vx) + Math.abs(e.vy) < 8) {\n      e.vy *= 1.05;\n      e.vx *= 1.05;\n    }\n    // :32857-32869 掷 1/200（专家 1/100）→ 8 速直冲玩家、转扑咬态\n    if (player && Math.floor(Math.random() * 200) === 0) {\n      const pdx = player.cx - e.cx, pdy = player.cy - e.cy;\n      const pd = Math.hypot(pdx, pdy) || 1;\n      e.vx = (pdx / pd) * 8;\n      e.vy = (pdy / pd) * 8;\n      e.ai0 = 1;\n    }\n    e.x += e.vx; e.y += e.vy;\n    return;\n  }\n  // ---- 扑咬态 ai[0]=1（:32871-32913）----\n  // :32887-32894 距主体 >700px → 回环绕\n  const bdx = boss.cx - e.cx, bdy = boss.cy - e.cy;\n  if (Math.hypot(bdx, bdy) > 700) {\n    e.ai0 = 0;\n  } else {\n    // :32897-32900 未受击保持直飞（经典档无追踪）\n    if (!e.justHit) { e.x += e.vx; e.y += e.vy; return; }\n    // :32901-32912 knockBackResist==0（本仓 = 抗性 1.0）→ 计数 ai[1]>5 回环绕；\n    //   否则一击即回（267 原值 0.8 走此支）\n    if ((e.def.knockbackResist ?? 0) >= 1) {\n      e.ai1 += 1;\n      if (e.ai1 > 5) e.ai0 = 0;\n    } else {\n      e.ai0 = 0;\n    }\n  }\n  e.x += e.vx;\n  e.y += e.vy;\n  void game;\n}"
}

// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
// DD2：脚本化 rand 宿主（rand 队列消费完回退 7——永不命中 0 分支）

interface Spy { spawned: number[]; counts: Map<number, number>; any: Set<number> }

function makeDD2Host(randQueue: number[], spy: Spy): OldOnesArmyHost {
  return {
    broadcast: () => {},
    spawnNpc: (id) => { spy.spawned.push(id); spy.counts.set(id, (spy.counts.get(id) ?? 0) + 1); },
    countNpcs: (id) => spy.counts.get(id) ?? 0,
    anyNpc: (id) => spy.any.has(id),
    crystalPoints: () => [],
    dropAtCrystal: () => {},
    arenaEnds: () => ({ left: { x: 0, y: 0 }, right: { x: 0, y: 0 } }),
    findGroundY: () => null,
    rand: (n) => {
      const v = randQueue.length ? randQueue.shift()! : 7;
      return v % n;
    },
    activePlayersCount: () => 1,   // 单机：:1267/:1578 缩放循环零次
  };
}

// 血肉墙 Boss 族 1:1 移植(Terarria1456 NPC.cs ilspycmd 反编译,经典模式参数):
//   aiStyle 27 血肉墙本体 113  → NPC.cs:25718-26140 → wallOfFleshAI
//   aiStyle 28 血肉墙之眼 114  → NPC.cs:26141-26298 → wofEyeAI
//   aiStyle 29 饥饿者   115  → NPC.cs:26299-26496 → hungryAI
//   (Hungry II 116 = aiStyle 2 飘浮眼,已有 floatEyeAI,不在此文件)
//
// 与原版的两处结构性差异(无 Main 静态/无 whoAmI 槽位):
//   1. 原版部件靠 Main.wofNPCIndex / realLife 锚定头(NewNPC 的 Start 只是槽位搜索
//      起点,NPC.cs:81524-81559 不写 ai[])→ 这里用闲置 ai 槽存头实体 id 锚定:
//      之眼 ai1(其状态机只占 ai0)、饥饿者 ai3(其 ai1 被原版用作脱落倒计时),
//      同 bossAI.ts primePartAI 的 find head 模式;头失效/死亡 → 部件自毁。
//   2. Main.wofDrawAreaTop/Bottom 是 Main 静态(同屏只有一只血肉墙)→ 模块级变量,
//      由墙初始化(localAI[0]==0 分支,:25726-25731)复位。
import type { Enemy } from './Enemy';
import type { GameHooks } from './types';
import type { Player } from './Player';
import { TILE } from '../core/constants';
import { spawnPart, shoot, losClear } from './bossAI';

// Main.wofDrawAreaTop/Bottom(NPC.cs Main 静态;单只血肉墙语义,墙初始化时复位 -1)
let wofDrawAreaTop = -1;
let wofDrawAreaBottom = -1;

// ---- 每实体辅助槽(原版 localAI[1..3],Enemy 只有 ai0-ai3 且 ai1/ai2 被 AI 主链占用) ----
interface WallLocals { despawnT: number; roarT: number }        // localAI[1] / localAI[3]
interface EyeLocals { charge: number; burst: number; lastHeadHp: number } // localAI[1]/[2] + 血量同步基线
const wallLocals = new WeakMap<Enemy, WallLocals>();
const eyeLocals = new WeakMap<Enemy, EyeLocals>();
const wallLocalOf = (e: Enemy): WallLocals => {
  let l = wallLocals.get(e);
  if (!l) { l = { despawnT: 0, roarT: 0 }; wallLocals.set(e, l); }
  return l;
};
const eyeLocalOf = (e: Enemy): EyeLocals => {
  let l = eyeLocals.get(e);
  if (!l) { l = { charge: 0, burst: 0, lastHeadHp: 0 }; eyeLocals.set(e, l); }
  return l;
};

/** 伤害/防御基值缓存(ai29 每 tick 从基值派生临时修正,防重复乘膨胀;同 bossAI.baseOf) */
const baseStats = new WeakMap<Enemy, { dmg: number; def: number }>();
const baseOf = (e: Enemy) => {
  let b = baseStats.get(e);
  if (!b) { b = { dmg: e.def.damage, def: e.def.defense ?? 0 }; baseStats.set(e, b); }
  return b;
};

/** 锚定槽:之眼用 ai1(其状态机只占 ai0),饥饿者用 ai3(其 ai1 被原版用作脱落倒计时) */
const headAnchor = (e: Enemy): number => (e.vanillaId === 115 ? e.ai3 : e.ai1);

/** 按锚定槽找血肉墙本体(原版 Main.wofNPCIndex;头失效 → 部件自毁) */
const headOf = (e: Enemy, game: GameHooks): Enemy | null => {
  const head = (game.enemies() as Enemy[]).find((n) => n.id === headAnchor(e) && !n.dead);
  if (!head || head.vanilla?.aiStyle !== 27) return null;
  return head;
};

const countType = (game: GameHooks, id: number): number =>
  (game.enemies() as Enemy[]).filter((n) => n.vanillaId === id && !n.dead).length; // 原版 CountNPCS

```

18 : 13（专家 ×1.5/大师 ×2 由调用侧难度档处理） */\nexport function beeRetaliateRoll(strongBees: boolean, rnd: () => number): { count: number; base: number } {\n  let count = 1;\n  if (Math.floor(rnd() * 3) === 0) count++;\n  if (Math.floor(rnd() * 3) === 0) count++;\n  if (strongBees && Math.floor(rnd() * 3) === 0) count++;\n  return { count, base: strongBees ? 7:export function VI(vid: number): string {
// 原版物品 id → 注册 key/内部 id 的统一查表入口(本地物品退役后全库唯一物品字面量源)。
// ★ 严禁手拼 vi_ 键字符串:蛇形/驼峰双注册下唯一权威是 VANILLA_ITEM_KEY_BY_ID(驼峰)。
// 后续 dual-key 清理(蛇形/驼峰归一)只需改本文件,消费点无感。
import { VANILLA_ITEM_KEY_BY_ID, ITEM_BY_KEY } from './items';

/** 原版物品 id → 注册 key(查不到返回 '') */
export function VI(vid: number): string {
  return VANILLA_ITEM_KEY_BY_ID[vid] ?? '';
}

/** 原版物品 id → 内部 id(查不到返回 0) */
export function VI_ID(vid: number): number {
  const k = VANILLA_ITEM_KEY_BY_ID[vid];
  return k !== undefined ? (ITEM_BY_KEY[k] ?? 0) : 0;
}

/** 高频消费点语义常量(grep 唯一入口;数值 = 原版 item id 权威) */

```

One more check — in the phase-1 branch I have:

```ts
if (e.ai0 === 0) {
  if (creepers.length === 0) { e.ai0 = -1; e.lai1 = 0; e.spawnAlpha = 0; }
  if (player) {
    e.lai1 += 1;
    if (e.lai1 >= 120 + ...) { ... e.ai0 = 1; ... }
  }
}
```

Vanilla order: count creepers → if 0 → ai[0]=-1, localAI[1]=0, alpha=0, netUpdate; THEN localAI[1] += 1 and teleport check (which sets ai[0]=1). So if creepers just hit 0, ai[0] = -1 and then the teleport check could overwrite ai[0] = 1 in the same tick! Vanilla has exactly this ordering (the teleport block is inside `if (this.ai[0] == 0f)` — wait, after setting ai[0] = -1, the `if (this.ai[0] == 0f)` was already entered (the branch was chosen before), so the teleport code inside still runs with ai[0] now -1... Actually looking at vanilla:

```csharp
if (this.ai[0] == 0f)
{
    if (Main.netMode != 1)
    {
        int num871 = 0; // count
        if (num871 == 0)
        {
            this.ai[0] = -1f;
            localAI[1] = 0f;
            alpha = 0;
            netUpdate = true;
        }
        localAI[1] += 1f;
        if (localAI[1] >= 120 + rand(300)) { ... this.ai[0] = 1f; ... }
    }
}
```

Yes — same tick: creepers 0 → ai[0]=-1 → then localAI[1]=0 +=1 = 1 → not ≥ threshold → stays -1 ✓. Only if the threshold were ≤1 would it overwrite (impossible: min 120). So vanilla-safe, and my implementation matches exactly (same ordering, same reset of lai1 to 0 before +=1) ✓.
